# java-se library index

Java SE API: the java.base and java.net.http modules, from the Javadoc of OpenJDK. Version 25, from <https://github.com/openjdk/jdk> at jdk-25-ga, commit 6c48f4ed707bf0b15f9b6098de30db8aae6fa40f.
License: GPL-2.0-only WITH Classpath-exception-2.0, by Oracle and/or its affiliates, and the OpenJDK contributors; see THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md. The text was converted to plain Markdown and cut into files of 16 KB at most.

This index is long: search it with grep for your words, never read it whole. Then open one file and read only the section you need.
